> **Corrected reruns complete:**618/618 NIR/DNVF fits now succeed, including all100 ACDC/NIR cases after the thin-volume NCC fix. Current scores are in the separate corrected-rerun table in [BASELINES_REPORT.md](BASELINES_REPORT.md).

> **Current correction status:** invalid NIR and TF32-enabled DNVF official results have been deleted and withdrawn from aggregation. Both full replacements are prepared, not launched. Other methods are preserved. See [NEURAL_RERUNS.md](NEURAL_RERUNS.md) and the current [BASELINES_REPORT.md](BASELINES_REPORT.md). Historical timing/diagnostic discussion below does not represent current competitive results.

# External baseline results

Central record of standardized comparisons on Neurite-OASIS, ACDC, LPBA40 and IXI.

<!-- BEGIN official_four_20261116 -->
## official_four_20261116

Running. Updated 2026-11-17 21:41:34 HKT.

1236 / 1236 fits finished, with 0 process failures.

One seed (42) per pair. Eight GPU workers and eight independent CPU workers, four native CPU threads per process.

[Frozen plan](../baselines/results/official_four_20261116/plan.json) · [Progress](../baselines/results/official_four_20261116/progress.json) · [Final summary](../baselines/results/official_four_20261116/summary.json)

All runs use standardized_v1. Dice is the equal foreground-label mean. HD95 uses prepared-grid mm. NPJ is a percentage. SDlogJ uses log(max(detJ,1e-9)). Jacobians use central differences with two boundary voxels excluded. IXI HD95 is reported in mm using nominal 1 × 1 × 1 mm benchmark spacing. Missing surfaces produce null HD95 and explicit scored counts.

## Neural baseline one-case profile — 2026-11-16

All four source-configured fits completed successfully on Neurite-OASIS pair 001, seed 42, one dedicated RTX 5090 (32 GB) per method, four concurrent fits. Runtime is subprocess wall time including startup, fitting, inference and standardized scoring. These are pilot timings, not cohort aggregate scores.

| Method | Runtime (s) | Peak allocated (GiB) | Peak reserved (GiB) | Sampled device peak (GiB) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| ccidir | 94.8 | 1.17 | 1.33 | 2.06 |
| nir | 32.1 | 1.61 | 1.74 | 2.48 |
| nodeo | 56.4 | 2.99 | 3.59 | 4.34 |
| dnvf | 93.3 | 25.80 | 29.96 | 30.71 |

Allocated/reserved peaks come from PyTorch; device memory was sampled every two seconds and includes CUDA overhead. DNVF fits but leaves little headroom; use one fit per GPU.

The full comparison has 309 pairs per method (Neurite-OASIS 49, ACDC 100, LPBA40 45, IXI 115), totaling 1,236 fits. Applying these Neurite timings to every pair gives 23.75 GPU-hours, or 2.97 hours with eight GPUs ideally utilized. Budget approximately 3–4 hours on eight RTX 5090s, allowing for contention and scheduling; this is a projection from one brain case, not a measured bound. Dataset sizes differ, especially ACDC. No full neural comparison was launched.

Raw measurements: [profile.json](../baselines/results/neural_neurite_profile_20261116/profile.json); exact commands: [plan.json](../baselines/results/neural_neurite_profile_20261116/plan.json).

## DNVF largest-case memory checks — 2026-11-16

Scanned NIfTI headers for all 309 official pairs and selected the maximum prepared voxel count per dataset, taking the first pair on ties. All brain cases tie within their dataset; 44 ACDC directed pairs tie at the maximum. All four tests used pair 001, seed 42, unchanged native 200-epoch source settings, and a dedicated RTX 5090 each. All completed fitting, inference and standardized scoring without OOM.

| Dataset | Prepared XYZ shape | Wall time (s) | Peak allocated (GiB) | Peak reserved (GiB) | Sampled device peak (GiB) |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| neurite_oasis | 160×192×224 | 93.0 | 25.80 | 29.96 | 30.71 |
| acdc | 128×128×32 | 13.4 | 2.04 | 2.23 | 2.98 |
| lpba40 | 160×192×160 | 66.4 | 18.45 | 21.39 | 22.14 |
| ixi | 160×192×224 | 92.1 | 25.80 | 29.96 | 30.71 |

Device usage sampled every 0.5 seconds; PyTorch peaks are allocator counters. Neurite/IXI device usage reached 31,451 MiB of 32,607 MiB reported capacity (about 1.13 GiB spare). Keep one fit per GPU with no competing CUDA workloads. This checks the largest tensor sizes; it does not guarantee every case succeeds.

[Raw profiles](../baselines/results/dnvf_largest_profile_20261116/profile.json), [complete shape inventory](../baselines/results/dnvf_largest_profile_20261116/shape_inventory.json), [commands and selected inputs](../baselines/results/dnvf_largest_profile_20261116/plan.json). No full-cohort run was launched.

## Neural external baselines — launched 2026-11-16

Launched **20:00 HKT, 16 November 2026**, in tmux session
`inr-neural-baselines`. Attach with `tmux attach -t inr-neural-baselines`.
All eight RTX 5090 GPUs run one fit per GPU from a shared queue.
Methods: **ccIDIR, NIR, NODEO, DNVF**, 309 fits each, **1,236 fits total**.
Each method covers Neurite-OASIS 49, ACDC 100, LPBA40 test 45 and IXI test 115.
Single seed 42; pinned author protocols and recorded localization adapters.
Dataset preparation is frozen: no preparation or resampling was invoked.
The run plan records preparation/configuration/evaluator hashes and the exact
ordered inputs. Resolved method configurations are copied into `configs/`.
All 1,236 planned jobs passed input-existence checks before launch.

Estimated duration **3–4 hours**, finish approximately **23:00 HKT today to
00:00 HKT on 17 November**. The one-Neurite-case measurements project 2.97 hours
at ideal eight-GPU utilization; this budget allows scheduling and contention.
Dataset-dependent times remain uncertain; largest-case DNVF tests passed on all
four cohorts, reaching 30.71 GiB device usage on Neurite/IXI. No competing GPU
jobs should be launched while this queue is active.

Run directory: `baselines/results/official_neural_20261116`.
[Plan](../baselines/results/official_neural_20261116/plan.json) ·
[Live progress](../baselines/results/official_neural_20261116/progress.json) ·
[Final summary, when complete](../baselines/results/official_neural_20261116/summary.json).
Per-case logs, metrics and checkpoints are retained. No dense maps or warped
images are persisted. Standardized Dice, HD95, NPJ (%) and SDlogJ aggregates,
scored denominators and failures are written by the launcher after every case.
The tmux pane remains available when the launcher exits, retaining exit status.
<!-- BEGIN official_neural_20261116 -->

## Completed neural external baselines — 2026-11-16

All 1,236/1,236 fits completed with zero process failures. Started 20:00 HKT; completed 22:16 HKT on 16 November (2 h 16 min). Eight RTX 5090 workers, one fit per GPU, single seed 42, frozen prepared datasets and pinned source recipes. No retries or score-based exclusions.

Diagnostic qualification: [NIR/DNVF audit](NEURAL_BASELINE_DIAGNOSTICS.md) identifies
a major NIR/LPBA40 normalization confound and widespread DNVF/IXI optimization
failures. Affected scores are provisional adaptation results; no reruns or
score replacements have been performed.

Controlled DNVF IXI pair66 with TF32 off: Dice0.740307 versus original0.057563; HD95 3.680531 versus23.658882; NPJ0% versus3.413493%. Full replacement of both methods is prepared:618 fits, heuristic ETA59.5min on8 GPUs. [Details](NEURAL_RERUNS.md).
